{\mathbb{E}}[|J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k}),ind}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})|^{2}]={\mathbb{E}}[|J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta)|^{2}]-\left|\frac{C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})}{C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}}\right|^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[|J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta^{\prime})|^{2}]=C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}-\frac{|C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})|^{2}}{C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}}.

Using this decomposition of
J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta)
and the measurability
of the different quantities with respect to
the
\sigma
-algebras of the form
\mathcal{G}_{j}
, we deduce that one can write:

\displaystyle(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})})}(\theta))_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}=(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},ind)}+E_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}})})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}},

(B.45)

with
(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},ind)})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
is a Gaussian process, independent of

(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})})}(\theta^{\prime}))_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
, and
distributed as
(\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}W_{\tau^{(\mathtt{k}_{+},\mathtt{k}_{+})}_{l}-\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}_{l}})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
with
\tau^{(\mathtt{k}_{+},\mathtt{k}_{+})}_{j}=\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{j-1}\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}(2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}+p)^{-1}
and

\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}_{l}:=\sum_{t=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{l-1}\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}\frac{|C_{t,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})|^{2}}{C_{t,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}}

and
(E_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}})})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
defined by

\displaystyle E_{2^{l+1}}^{(2^{l})}=\Re\Bigg(\sigma\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}e^{i\psi_{2^{l}+p2^{l-\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}}(\theta)}\frac{C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})}{C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}}J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta^{\prime})\Bigg).

(B.46)

Note that
\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}_{l}
and
E_{2^{l+1}}^{(2^{l})}
here represent quantities which are different from those denoted in the same way in the proof of Lemma
B.4
.
Furthermore notice that

Fact 1:

For any
l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}
,

\displaystyle|E_{2^{l+1}}^{(2^{l})}|\leq|E|_{2^{l+1}}^{(2^{l})}:=\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}|C_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})|(2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}+p)|J_{l,p}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta^{\prime})|

is measurable with respect to the sigma field
\sigma\left({\mathcal{N}}_{t}^{\mathbb{C}},\,t\in[|2^{l},2^{l+1}-1|]\right)
.

Fact 2:

The process
(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},ind)})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
is independent of the couple
(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})})}(\theta^{\prime}),|E|_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}})})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
.

Fact 3:

\sup_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}|\mathtt{C}_{l}^{(\mathtt{k})}|\ll 2^{-\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}
if
r
is large enough. Indeed we have

\displaystyle\sup_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}|\mathtt{C}_{l}^{(\mathtt{k})}|

\displaystyle\ll\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{\infty}\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}(2^{\mathtt{k}-l})^{2}(2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}+p)\ll\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{\infty}2^{2\mathtt{k}-2l+2\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}\ll\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}+3\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}^{\infty}2^{2(\mathtt{k}+3\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}-l)-4\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}\ll 2^{-2\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}.

It means that the process
(R_{2^{l}}^{(2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}},ind)})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
is very ”similar” to
\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}(W_{\tau_{l}^{(\mathtt{k}_{+},\mathtt{k}_{+})}})_{l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}
.

Fact 4:

|E|
is small. For any
m\geq 0
,
l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}
, we introduce the event

\mathtt{E}_{m}^{(l)}:=\{|E|_{2^{l+1}}^{(2^{l})}\geq 2^{-\frac{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}{4}}m\}\ .

For some universal constants
c,c^{\prime}>0
, and
m\geq 1/2
, the probability of
\mathtt{E}_{m}^{(l)}
is smaller than

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(|E|_{2^{l+1}}^{2^{l}}\geq 2^{-\frac{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}{4}}m\right)\leq 2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}{\mathbb{P}}\left(c2^{\mathtt{k}-l}|J_{l,0}^{(\mathtt{k})}(\theta^{\prime})|\geq m2^{-\frac{9}{4}\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}\right)

\displaystyle\ll 2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}e^{-c^{\prime}m^{2}2^{2(l-\mathtt{k}-\frac{7}{4}\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})})}}

\displaystyle\ll e^{-c^{\prime}m^{2}2^{2(l-\mathtt{k}-2\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})})}}.

Then for
r
large and
l\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}
,

{\mathbb{P}}\left(|E|_{2^{l+1}}^{2^{l}}\geq 2^{-\frac{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}{4}}m\right)\ll e^{-m^{2}2^{l-\mathtt{k}_{+}}}\ .

Using the decomposition (
B.45
) and Fact 2 and noticing that
\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{N}m2^{-\frac{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}{4}}\leq m2^{-\frac{r}{400}}
for
r
large enough, we can affirm that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{N}^{(\Delta,\kappa)}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})

(B.47)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m\geq 0}{\mathbb{P}}\left(Ev(r,1,E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x,z),\,\cup_{j\in[|\mathtt{k}_{+},N-1|]}\mathtt{E}_{m}^{(j)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\sup_{l_{\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{(N)}-1\leq x^{\prime}+w^{\prime}\leq u_{\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{(N)}+1}{\mathbb{P}}\left(Ev(\mathtt{k}_{+},1+(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}},\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}+E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x^{\prime}+w^{\prime},z^{\prime})\right).

Here, by abuse of notation, we refer to the same event
Ev(k,a,E,z)
as in Eq. (
B.3.1
) but for the new time clock
\tau_{.}^{(\mathtt{k}_{+},\mathtt{k}_{+})}
. By the same arguments used to prove (
B.39
) we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(Ev(r,1,E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x,z),\,\cup_{j\in[|\mathtt{k}_{+},N-1|]}\mathtt{E}_{m}^{(j)}\right)\ll_{\upsilon}e^{2(x-z)}|xz|2^{r-N_{+}-(m^{2}/8)}.

(B.48)

On the other hand, by using the inequality (
B.26
) deduced from the Girsanov transform (which still holds for the time clock
\tau^{(\mathtt{k}_{+},\mathtt{k}_{+})}_{j}=\sum_{l=\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{j-1}\sum_{p=0}^{2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}-1}(2^{\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}+p)^{-1}
), we obtain for any
x^{\prime}+w^{\prime}\in[l_{\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{(N)}-1,u_{\mathtt{k}_{+}}^{(N)}+1]
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(Ev(\mathtt{k}_{+},1+(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}},\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}+E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x^{\prime}+w^{\prime},z^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle\ll_{\upsilon}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N_{+}}e^{1+2(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}}}N^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{2(x^{\prime}+w^{\prime}-z^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\qquad\times{\mathbb{P}}\left(GEv(\mathtt{k}_{+},1+2(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}},\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}+E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x^{\prime}+w^{\prime},z^{\prime}))\right),

(B.49)

where we used that
\sup_{j\geq\mathtt{k}_{+}}|\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}_{j}|\leq 2^{-\kappa^{(\mathtt{k})}}\leq(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}}
. We bound the probability
of the
GEv
event by
1
and use the fact that

x^{\prime}+w^{\prime}\leq 1+u_{k_{0}}^{(N)}\leq 1-(N_{+}-\mathtt{k}_{+})^{\alpha_{-}}-\frac{3}{4}\log N
.
We then get

\displaystyle P\left(Ev(\mathtt{k}_{+},1+(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}},\mathtt{C}^{(\mathtt{k})}+E^{(\mathtt{k}_{+})},x^{\prime}+w^{\prime},z^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle\ll 2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N_{+}}e^{2(m+1)2^{-\frac{r}{400}}}N^{\frac{3}{2}}e^{2(x^{\prime}+w^{\prime}-z^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\ll_{\upsilon}2^{-2z^{\prime}}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N_{+}}e^{2m+2}N^{3/2}e^{-2(N_{+}-\mathtt{k}_{+})^{\alpha_{-}}-\frac{3}{2}\log N}

\displaystyle\ll 2^{-2z^{\prime}}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N_{+}}e^{2m+2}e^{-(N_{+}-\mathtt{k}_{+})^{\alpha_{-}}},

Finally, by combining this equation with (
B.48
) and (
B.47
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{N}^{(\Delta,\kappa)}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})

\displaystyle\ll_{\upsilon}|xz|2^{2(x-z-z^{\prime})}2^{r-N_{+}}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N}e^{-(N_{+}-\mathtt{k}_{+})^{\alpha_{-}}}\sum_{m\geq 0}e^{2m+2-(m^{2}/8)}

\displaystyle\ll|xz|2^{2(x-z-z^{\prime})}2^{r-N_{+}}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-N}e^{-(N_{+}-\mathtt{k}_{+})^{\alpha_{-}}}\ ,

which concludes the proof of Lemma
B.6
.
∎

B.4
Short time barriers

We will also need the analogue of Lemmas
B.4
and
B.5

with
\mathfrak{I}_{N}
replaced by
\mathfrak{I}_{t,f}
. Thus, let

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{t,f}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})={\mathbb{P}}_{t,f}(\theta,\theta^{\prime},x,z,x^{\prime},z^{\prime}):={\mathbb{P}}(\mathfrak{I}_{t,f}(\theta,x,z)\cap\mathfrak{I}_{t,f}(\theta^{\prime},x^{\prime},z^{\prime})).

(B.50)

We have the following.

Lemma B.8

(Time of branching
\mathtt{k}\leq N/2
)
.

For any
\upsilon\in(0,1)
,
r
large enough,
C>1
,

t
large enough depending on
r
,
z,z^{\prime}\in[-\sqrt{t}/C,C\sqrt{t}]
,

\mathtt{k}\leq t
and
2^{-\mathtt{k}}\leq\frac{||\theta-\theta^{\prime}||}{2\pi}<2^{-(\mathtt{k}-1)}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{t,f}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})

\displaystyle\ll\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\frac{|zz^{\prime}xx^{\prime}|}{t^{3}}2^{2r-2t+2(x-z+x^{\prime}-z^{\prime})},&\text{when }\mathtt{k}_{+}\leq r,\\
\frac{|zz^{\prime}x|}{t^{3/2}}e^{2(x-z-z^{\prime})}2^{r-t}2^{\mathtt{k}_{+}-t}e^{-\mathtt{k}_{+}^{\alpha_{-}}},&\text{when }\mathtt{k}_{+}\geq r.\end{array}\right.

Lemma B.9

(Time of branching
\mathtt{k}\leq r/2
)
.

For any
\upsilon\in(0,1)
,
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
large enough,
r
large enough,
C>1
,

t
large enough depending on
r
,
z,z^{\prime}\in[-\sqrt{t}/C,C\sqrt{t}]
,

\mathtt{k}\leq\frac{r}{2}
,
2^{-\mathtt{k}}\leq\frac{||\theta-\theta^{\prime}||}{2\pi}\leq 2^{-(\mathtt{k}-1)}
and
t
large enough depending on
r
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{t,f}(\theta,\theta^{\prime})

\displaystyle\leq(1+\eta_{r,\upsilon})2^{2r-2t}e^{2(x-z+x^{\prime}-z^{\prime})}{\mathbb{P}}(Event_{r,N}(x,z)){\mathbb{P}}(Event_{r,N}(x^{\prime},z^{\prime})),

where

\underset{\upsilon\rightarrow 0}{\limsup}\,\underset{r\rightarrow\infty}{\limsup}\,\eta_{r,\upsilon}=0.

Appendix C
Classical estimates on Gaussian walks

The following estimates are classical and extend those in

[
CMN18
]
by keeping track of the dependence in starting and ending points.
In the following, the process
\left(W_{s};s\in{\mathbb{R}}_{+}\right)
is a standard Brownian motion. We use the notation from
B.2
.

The following is classical. Since the proof is short, we bring it.

Lemma C.1
.

Notation
as in Section

B.2
, with
x,z
in the appropriate range.
Then,

(1-\eta_{N,r,k_{1},v})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|xz|v\leq N^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathbb{P}}(Event_{r,N})\leq(1+\eta_{N,r,k_{1},v})\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|xz|v,

(C.1)

where

\lim_{v\to 0}\lim_{r\to\infty}\lim_{k_{1}\to\infty}\lim_{N\to\infty}\eta_{N,r,k_{1}}=0.

Proof.

We show the lower bound, the upper bound is similar.
Recall that

\displaystyle Event_{r,N}=Event_{r,N}(x,z)\coloneqq

\displaystyle\left\{\forall j\in[|r,N_{+}|],\,l_{j}^{(N)}\leq x+\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}W_{\tau^{(r)}_{j}}\leq u_{j}^{(N)},x+\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}W_{\tau^{(r)}_{N_{+}}}\in\tau^{(r)}_{N_{+}}-\frac{3}{4}\log N+[z,z+\upsilon)\right\}.

For
r,k_{1}
large, we have that
|\tau^{(r)}_{j}-(j-r)\log 2|\leq 1
.
Set, comparing with (
B.2
) and (
B.2
), for
k\in[r,N_{+}+1]
,

\displaystyle u_{k}^{+,(N)}\coloneqq-2+\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}-k^{2\alpha_{-}},&\text{if }k\leq\lfloor N/2\rfloor,\\
-(N-k)^{2\alpha_{-}}-\frac{3}{4}\log N,&\text{if }\lfloor N/2\rfloor<k\leq N,\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle l_{k}^{+,(N)}\coloneqq 2+\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}-k^{\alpha_{+}-\alpha_{-}},&\text{if }k\leq\lfloor N/2\rfloor,\\
-(N-k)^{\alpha_{+}-\alpha_{-}}-\frac{3}{4}\log N,&\text{if }\lfloor N/2\rfloor<k\leq N.\end{array}\right.

The crucial fact is that for
N
large we have that for all
k\in[r,N_{+}]
,

\inf_{\theta\in[(k-1)\vee r,k+1]}u_{k}^{(N)}\geq u_{k}^{+,(N)},\quad\sup_{\theta\in[(k-1)\vee r,k+1]}l_{k}^{(N)}\leq l_{k}^{+,(N)}.

(C.6)

In particular, we have that

\displaystyle Event_{r,N}^{\prime}\coloneqq

\displaystyle\left\{\forall t\in[r,\tau^{(r)}_{N_{+}}],\,l_{t}^{+,(N)}\leq x+\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}W_{t}\leq u_{t}^{+,(N)},x+\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}}W_{\tau^{(r)}_{N_{+}}}\in\tau^{(r)}_{N_{+}}-\frac{3}{4}\log N+[z,z+\upsilon)\right\}

\displaystyle\subset Event_{r,N}.

The conclusion of the lemma follows from a variant of

[
BRZ19
, Lemma 2.1]
, and our assumptions on
x,z
.
∎
